    16	namespace Eccube\Twig\Loader;
    17	
    18	use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\RequestStack;
    19	use Twig\Loader\FilesystemLoader;
    20	use Twig\Source;
    21	
    22	/**
    23	 * FilesystemLoaderを拡張し、ロケールに応じたテンプレート切り替えを行う
    24	 *
    25	 * - リクエストのロケールに応じて 'Product/detail.{locale}.twig' を優先して読み込む
    26	 *   - 例: ja → detail.ja.twig / en → detail.en.twig
    27	 * - ロケール付きテンプレートが存在しない場合、'Product/detail.twig' にフォールバックする
    28	 */
    29	class LocaleFilesystemLoader extends FilesystemLoader
    30	{
    31	    private ?RequestStack $requestStack = null;
    32	    private ?string $defaultLocale = null;
    33	
    34	    public function setRequestStack(RequestStack $requestStack): void
    35	    {
    36	        $this->requestStack = $requestStack;
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    public function setDefaultLocale(string $defaultLocale): void
    40	    {
    41	        $this->defaultLocale = $defaultLocale;
    42	    }
    43	
    44	    /**
    45	     * @see FilesystemLoader::exists()
    46	     */
    47	    #[\Override]
    48	    public function exists(string $name): bool
    49	    {
    50	        $localeTemplateName = $this->getLocaleTemplateName($name);
    51	
    52	        if ($localeTemplateName === null) {
    53	            return parent::exists($name);
    54	        }
    55	
    56	        if (parent::exists($localeTemplateName)) {
    57	            return true;
    58	        }
    59	
    60	        return parent::exists($name);
    61	    }
    62	
    63	    /**
    64	     * @see FilesystemLoader::getSourceContext()
    65	     */
    66	    #[\Override]
    67	    public function getSourceContext(string $name): Source
    68	    {
    69	        $localeTemplateName = $this->getLocaleTemplateName($name);
    70	
    71	        if ($localeTemplateName === null) {
    72	            return parent::getSourceContext($name);
    73	        }
    74	
    75	        if (parent::exists($localeTemplateName)) {
    76	            return parent::getSourceContext($localeTemplateName);
    77	        }
    78	
    79	        return parent::getSourceContext($name);
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    /**
    83	     * @see FilesystemLoader::getCacheKey()
    84	     */
    85	    #[\Override]
    86	    public function getCacheKey(string $name): string
    87	    {
    88	        $localeTemplateName = $this->getLocaleTemplateName($name);
    89	
    90	        if ($localeTemplateName === null) {
    91	            return parent::getCacheKey($name);
    92	        }
    93	
    94	        if (parent::exists($localeTemplateName)) {
    95	            return parent::getCacheKey($localeTemplateName);
    96	        }
    97	
    98	        return parent::getCacheKey($name);
    99	    }
   100	
   101	    /**
   102	     * @see FilesystemLoader::isFresh()
   103	     */
   104	    #[\Override]
   105	    public function isFresh(string $name, int $time): bool
   106	    {
   107	        $localeTemplateName = $this->getLocaleTemplateName($name);
   108	
   109	        if ($localeTemplateName === null) {
   110	            return parent::isFresh($name, $time);
   111	        }
   112	
   113	        if (parent::exists($localeTemplateName)) {
   114	            return parent::isFresh($localeTemplateName, $time);
   115	        }
   116	
   117	        return parent::isFresh($name, $time);
   118	    }
   119	
   120	    /**
   121	     * ロケール付きのテンプレート名を返す(解決できない場合は null を返す)
   122	     */
   123	    private function getLocaleTemplateName(string $name): ?string
   124	    {
   125	        if ($this->requestStack === null || $this->defaultLocale === null) {
   126	            return null;
   127	        }
   128	
   129	        $request = $this->requestStack->getCurrentRequest();
   130	
   131	        if ($request === null) {
   132	            return null;
   133	        }
   134	
   135	        // 該当するテンプレートが存在しない場合は null を返す
   136	        if (!str_ends_with($name, '.twig')) {
   137	            return null;
   138	        }
   139	
   140	        $locale = $request->getLocale();
   141	        $localeSuffix = '.'.$locale.'.twig';
   142	
   143	        // 既に当該ロケール付き (foo.ja.twig 等) なら null を返す
   144	        if (str_ends_with($name, $localeSuffix)) {
   145	            return null;
   146	        }
   147	
   148	        // ロケール付きのテンプレート名を返す
   149	        return substr($name, 0, -strlen('.twig')).$localeSuffix;
   150	    }
   151	}
   255	
   256	    #[\Override]
   257	    protected function build(ContainerBuilder $container): void
   258	    {
   259	        $this->addEntityExtensionPass($container);
   260	
   261	        $container->registerExtension(new EccubeExtension());
   262	
   263	        // サービスタグの自動設定を行う
   264	        $container->addCompilerPass(new AutoConfigurationTagPass(), PassConfig::TYPE_BEFORE_OPTIMIZATION, 11);
   265	
   266	        // サービスタグの収集より先に実行し, 付与されているタグをクリアする.
   267	        // FormPassは優先度0で実行されているので, それより速いタイミングで実行させる.
   268	        // 自動登録されるタグやコンパイラパスの登録タイミングは, FrameworkExtension::load(), FrameworkBundle::build()を参考に.
   269	        $container->addCompilerPass(new PluginPass(), PassConfig::TYPE_BEFORE_OPTIMIZATION, 10);
   270	
   271	        // DocumentRootをルーティディレクトリに設定する.
   272	        $container->addCompilerPass(new WebServerDocumentRootPass('%kernel.project_dir%/'));
   273	
   274	        // twigのurl,path関数を差し替え
   275	        $container->addCompilerPass(new TwigExtensionPass());
   276	
   277	        // ロケールに応じたTwigテンプレート切り替え（*.en.twig形式）
   278	        $container->addCompilerPass(new LocaleTwigLoaderPass());
   279	
   280	        // クエリカスタマイズの拡張.
   281	        $container->registerForAutoconfiguration(QueryCustomizer::class)
   282	            ->addTag(QueryCustomizerPass::QUERY_CUSTOMIZER_TAG);
   283	        $container->addCompilerPass(new QueryCustomizerPass());
   284	
   285	        // 管理画面ナビの拡張
   286	        $container->registerForAutoconfiguration(EccubeNav::class)
   287	            ->addTag(NavCompilerPass::NAV_TAG);
   288	        $container->addCompilerPass(new NavCompilerPass());
   289	
   290	        // TwigBlockの拡張
   291	        $container->registerForAutoconfiguration(EccubeTwigBlock::class)
   292	            ->addTag(TwigBlockPass::TWIG_BLOCK_TAG);
   293	        $container->addCompilerPass(new TwigBlockPass());

# 実装乖離監査 判定規約（全設計書共通）

調査全体の観点は [AUDIT_SCOPE.md](AUDIT_SCOPE.md) を参照。本書はその観点で判定するための手順を定める。

正本は `excel_to_html/output/*.html` から機械抽出した `design_audit/<doc>/sheets/<sheet-id>.txt` と
`design_audit/<doc>/images/<sheet-id>_imgN.png` だけ。実装は `../ec-cube-enterprise`（以下 ee）の HEAD。
**正本に書かれていないことは指摘にできない。実装を読まずに判定を書いてはいけない。**

## 判定値

| 判定 | 意味 |
| --- | --- |
| MATCHED | 設計どおり実装されている |
| DRIFT | 実装はあるが設計と違う |
| NOT_IMPLEMENTED | 設計の要求に対応する実装が無い |
| DESIGN_ISSUE | 正本内部で記述が矛盾し、どちらが正か決められない（設計裁定待ち） |
| UNVERIFIABLE | 実データ・実行時挙動に依存し静的解析では確定できない（理由必須） |
| OUT_OF_SCOPE | 実装対象の記述ではない（見出し・凡例・前置き・他機能の記述など。理由必須） |

## 機械ゲート（`audit_part.py check` が落とす。通らないものは成果物にできない）

1. DRIFT / NOT_IMPLEMENTED / DESIGN_ISSUE は `設計根拠_引用` が**当該シート本文に逐語で存在**すること
   （空白を詰めた部分一致で照合）。正本に無い要求は指摘にできない。
2. DRIFT / NOT_IMPLEMENTED は `実装参照` が ee 内に**実在するファイル:行**であること。行数超過も落ちる。
3. **MATCHED も実装の位置（`src/...php:123` 形式）を `判定根拠` か `実装参照` に最低1つ書くこと。**
   「設計どおり」と言う以上、どこを見たのかを示す。実在しない引用は落ちる。
4. DRIFT / NOT_IMPLEMENTED は `重要度`(P1/P2/P3)・`指摘区分`(未実装/実装違い)・`乖離種別`(IO/ふるまい)・
   `設計期待値`・`実装実態`・**`根本原因`** が必須。
5. 全行に `判定根拠` と `確信度`(high/med/low)。**そのシートの全要求に判定が要る（判定漏れ0）。**

## 過剰指摘を出さない（最重要・利用者指示 2026-08-21）

**1つの実装欠陥は1件として書く。** 設計書は同じ1つの欠陥を、項目表・機能仕様・現行仕様・入出力の
各所で繰り返し要求していることが多い。要求の数だけ指摘に割ると、実態より何倍も多い件数になる。

そのために `根本原因` 列がある。**同じ実装欠陥から出た指摘には、まったく同じ短いキーを書く。**
キーは設計書1冊の中で一意にする（シートをまたいで同じ欠陥なら同じキーにする）。
build はこのキーで1件に畳み、畳んだ要求IDは代表行に全件併記する（黙って落とさない）。

良い例:
- 振替明細に商品名・基準価格・在庫数・登録日・更新日・登録者・更新者が保存されない7件
  → すべて `根本原因 = 振替明細に振替当時の値を保存していない`
- 出庫日・入庫日が一覧でもCSVでも常に空になる7件
  → すべて `根本原因 = 出庫日時・入庫日時を書き込む処理が無い`
- 項目名の文言差が1画面に4件
  → すべて `根本原因 = 在庫一覧の項目名に商品管理側の共通文言を流用している`

キーは「実装のどこが1箇所直れば全部消えるか」で決める。**直す単位が別なら別のキーにする。**
迷ったら、その指摘を消すために触るファイルと行が同じかどうかで判断する。

**件数を稼がない。** 指摘の多さは成果ではない。同じことを言い換えて増やす、
影響の無い差を並べる、1つの原因を分割する——いずれも成果物の信頼を落とす。

## 指摘にしないもの（利用者指示・機械でも一部強制）

- **★書きの識別IDずれ**: カスタマイズ説明の「識別ID:N「X」を追加」が項目表とずれている件。
  追加すべき列は項目表側で決まり実装は追随済み。OUT_OF_SCOPE。
- **実装手段の差**: 通信方式・認可設定・受付先・内部配線・検証順序・トランザクション境界・
  セッションキー・ルート名・join の種類・クラス名/メソッド名など。`設計期待値` をこれらの語で書くと落ちる。
  結果・ふるまいの言葉で書けないなら、その指摘は取り下げる。
- **実装違いは I/O とふるまいに限る**: 応答にも画面にも現れない内部手順（読み込む設定値の内訳、
  ソルトの作り方など）は OUT_OF_SCOPE。
- **現行仕様に書かれた現行ソースの不具合・不用意な副作用**（0件でも見出し行だけのCSVを出す等）は
  踏襲対象ではない。ただし機能として定義された挙動（警告表示・並び順・据え置き時間等）は要求として扱う。
- **現行仕様と★カスタマイズ/リニューアル後の仕様が食い違うとき**は後者が優先。現行仕様側は OUT_OF_SCOPE。
  理由を「リニューアル後の仕様が優先するため踏襲対象ではない」と明記する。
- **見出し・表の見出し行・区分行・前置き・運用想定・背景説明**は OUT_OF_SCOPE。理由を書く。

## DESIGN_ISSUE にするもの

項目表・レイアウト図・機能仕様・現行仕様のあいだで記述が食い違い、**どちらが正か決められない**もの**だけ**。
実装と突き合わせる前に設計裁定が要る。`実装実態` は空にする（成果物には載らず、件数だけ残る）。

## 重要度（業務影響で決める。機能名で機械的に決めない）

- **P1**: 業務が回らず商売が止まる／データ整合性が壊れる／手作業でも代替できない。
  受注・買取・出荷・商品金額・金額相当（ポイント付与/失効）・決済・在庫・メール通知・
  外部連携（スマレジ/ユニサーチ/プリンター）・認証・認可・セキュリティ・法令要件、
  および登録フォームのバリデーション（そもそも登録できない）。
- **P2**: 迂回すれば回る（手作業・再実行・別経路）。CSV一括登録・イベント・デッキ・分析・コンテンツ。
- **P3**: 業務は回る。見出し・ボタン文言・列名の相違／並び順・桁区切りの相違。

例外は影響と迂回可否で判断する（受注管理でも文言差は P3、CSV一括登録でも取り込めない＝データ登録不能なら P1）。

## 画像を見ること

正本の文言はレイアウト図の画像に入っていることが多い。**画像を見ずに「実装が無い」と判定すると誤る。**
`design_audit/<doc>/images/<sheet-id>_imgN.png` を読み、根拠にしたときは `画像確認メモ` に書く。

## 手順

1. `design_audit/<doc>/sheets/<sheet-id>.txt` を全部読む。画像があれば全部見る。
2. `requirements.tsv` から当該シートの要求を全件取り出す。
3. ee の該当実装（コントローラ・フォーム・Twig・リポジトリ・サービス・locale）を読む。
   **grep の空振りを実在の否定に使わない**（同じ検索でも取りこぼすことがある。別の綴り・別の命名でも引く）。
4. `design_audit/<doc>/parts/<sheet-id>.tsv` に12列すべてを書く（TSV・ヘッダ付き）。
5. `python3 audit_part.py check --doc <doc> --sheet <sheet-id>` が OK になるまで直す。

## やってはいけないこと

- 実装を読まずに MATCHED を並べる（ゲート3で落ちるが、実在する無関係な行を引いて通すのは**捏造**）。
- 迷ったから OUT_OF_SCOPE にする（迷ったら UNVERIFIABLE か DESIGN_ISSUE。理由を書く）。
- 正本の文言を要約して `設計根拠_引用` に書く（**逐語**でないと落ちる）。

## 偽陽性率の抜き取り検査（設計書1冊ごと）

集約後の掲載予定の指摘から、P1・P2・P3 を各5件、決定的な手順（等間隔抽出。乱数を使わない）で選び、
**反証専任のエージェント**に1件1体で投げる。指示は「裏づけるな、壊しにかかれ。迷ったら取り下げ寄りに倒せ」。
潰す観点は5つ:

1. 設計根拠_引用が正本にそう書かれているか。文脈を切って意味を変えていないか。
   同じシートのリニューアル後の仕様・★カスタマイズがその要求を打ち消していないか。
2. 実装が本当に無いか／違うか。別ファイル・別命名・別階層を自分で探したか（grepは3回以上引く）。
3. 実装手段の差を指摘にしていないか。
4. 画面にも応答にも出ない内部挙動を指摘にしていないか。
5. 重要度が業務影響として妥当か。過大な重要度は過剰指摘と同じ害がある。

**取り下げ率が2割を超えたら、その設計書の判定は基準が緩い。規約を締めて判定からやり直す。**
1〜2件なら該当箇所を直して先へ進む。結果は AUDIT_PROGRESS.md に冊ごとに記録する。

スキーマの注意: 構造化出力のプロパティ名に日本語を使うと API が 400 を返す（`^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]{1,64}$`）。
最上位のキーは ASCII にすること。

    16	namespace Eccube\Service\Front\Event;
    17	
    18	use Eccube\Entity\BaseInfo;
    19	use Eccube\Entity\DtbEvent;
    20	use Eccube\Entity\DtbEventDetail;
    21	use Eccube\Entity\Master\MtbFormat;
    22	use Eccube\Repository\DtbEventRepository;
    23	use Eccube\Repository\Master\MtbHolidayRepository;
    24	
    25	/**
    26	 * イベント大会TOP のイベントスケジュール JSON shape を構築する。
    27	 * フロント側ビルド済 JS (hareruya-event.js) がブラウザから fetch する JSON endpoint で利用される。
    28	 * 文字列日付 format はフロント側期待の "Y/m/d H:i:s" / "Y/m/d" (holiday) に合わせる。
    29	 */
    30	final readonly class EventScheduleJsonBuilder
    31	{
    32	    private const DATETIME_FORMAT = 'Y/m/d H:i:s';
    33	
    34	    /**
    35	     * DB の mtb_event_scale.id → デザイン bundle が想定する event_scale_id のマッピング。
    36	     * 設計書では 1=おすすめ / 2=定番 / 3=特別 / 4=大型 だが、bundle 側は 1=大型 / 2=特別 / 4=おすすめ / else=デイリー を前提とするため変換する。
    37	     */
    38	    private const SCALE_ID_DB_TO_BUNDLE = [
    39	        4 => 1, // 大型
    40	        3 => 2, // 特別
    41	        1 => 4, // おすすめ
    42	        // DB id=2 (定番) は bundle 側で else (= デイリー) 扱いになるため変換テーブルから除外
    43	    ];
    44	
    45	    public function __construct(
    46	        private DtbEventRepository $eventRepository,
    47	        private MtbHolidayRepository $holidayRepository,
    48	    ) {
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    /**
    52	     * 指定店舗・指定日付範囲の dummy 互換 JSON shape を返す。
    53	     *
    54	     * @return array<string, mixed>
    55	     */
    56	    public function build(BaseInfo $Shop, \DateTimeImmutable $rangeStart, \DateTimeImmutable $rangeEnd, string $locale): array
    57	    {
    58	        $isEnglish = $locale === 'en';
    59	        $Events = $this->eventRepository->getEventsByShopAndDateRange($Shop->getId(), $rangeStart, $rangeEnd);
    60	
    61	        $detailIds = [];
    62	        foreach ($Events as $Event) {
    63	            foreach ($Event->getDetails() as $Detail) {
    64	                $detailIds[] = $Detail->getId();
    65	            }
    66	        }
    67	        $entryCounts = $this->eventRepository->countActiveEntriesByDetailIds($detailIds);
    68	
    69	        $eventPayloads = [];
    70	        foreach ($Events as $Event) {
    71	            $eventPayloads[] = $this->buildEvent($Event, $entryCounts, $isEnglish);
    72	        }
    73	
    74	        return [
    75	            'shop_id' => $Shop->getId(),
    76	            'shop_name_jp' => $Shop->getShopName(),
    77	            'shop_name_en' => $Shop->getShopNameEng() ?? $Shop->getShopName(),
    78	            'date_from' => $rangeStart->format(self::DATETIME_FORMAT),
    79	            'date_to' => $rangeEnd->format(self::DATETIME_FORMAT),
    80	            'events' => $eventPayloads,
    81	            'holidays' => $this->buildHolidays($rangeStart, $rangeEnd),
    82	        ];
    83	    }
    84	
    85	    /**
    86	     * @return list<array{holiday: string}>
    87	     */
    88	    private function buildHolidays(\DateTimeImmutable $rangeStart, \DateTimeImmutable $rangeEnd): array
    89	    {
    90	        $dateKeys = $this->holidayRepository->getDateKeysByRange($rangeStart, $rangeEnd);
    91	
    92	        $result = [];
    93	        foreach ($dateKeys as $dateKey) {
    94	            $result[] = ['holiday' => str_replace('-', '/', $dateKey)];
    95	        }
    96	
    97	        return $result;
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    /**
   101	     * @param array<int, int> $entryCounts detail_id => active entry count
   102	     *
   103	     * @return array<string, mixed>
   104	     */
   105	    private function buildEvent(DtbEvent $Event, array $entryCounts, bool $isEnglish): array
   106	    {
   107	        $Formats = [];
   108	        foreach ($Event->getFormats() as $Format) {
   109	            $Formats[] = $this->buildFormat($Format, $isEnglish);
   110	        }
   111	
   112	        $Details = [];
   113	        foreach ($Event->getDetails() as $Detail) {
   114	            $Details[] = $this->buildDetail($Detail, $entryCounts);
   115	        }
   116	
   117	        $EventScale = $Event->getEventScale();
   118	        $dbScaleId = $EventScale?->getId();
   119	        $bundleScaleId = $dbScaleId !== null ? (self::SCALE_ID_DB_TO_BUNDLE[$dbScaleId] ?? null) : null;
   120	        $Rel = $Event->getRel();
   121	
   122	        return [
   123	            'id' => $Event->getId(),
   124	            'name_jp' => $isEnglish ? ($Event->getNameEn() ?: $Event->getNameJp()) : $Event->getNameJp(),
   125	            'name_en' => $Event->getNameEn(),
   126	            'event_scale_id' => $bundleScaleId,
   127	            'rel_id' => $Rel->getId(),
   128	            'rel_name_jp' => $isEnglish ? ($Rel->getNameEn() ?: $Rel->getNameJp()) : $Rel->getNameJp(),
   129	            'rel_name_en' => $Rel->getNameEn(),
   130	            'entry_fee' => $Event->getEntryFee(),
   131	            'formats' => $Formats,
   132	            'details' => $Details,
   133	        ];
   134	    }
   135	
   136	    /**
   137	     * @return array<string, mixed>
   138	     */
   139	    private function buildFormat(MtbFormat $Format, bool $isEnglish): array
   140	    {
   141	        return [
   142	            'format_id' => $Format->getId(),
   143	            'format_name_jp' => $isEnglish ? ($Format->getNameEn() ?: $Format->getNameJp()) : $Format->getNameJp(),
   144	            'format_name_en' => $Format->getNameEn(),
   145	        ];
   146	    }
   147	
   148	    /**
   149	     * @param array<int, int> $entryCounts detail_id => active entry count
   150	     *
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	declare(strict_types=1);
     4

    16	namespace Eccube\Service\Front\Event;
    17	
    18	use Eccube\Entity\DtbEventDetail;
    19	
    20	final readonly class EventDetailButtonStateResolver
    21	{
    22	    public function resolve(
    23	        DtbEventDetail $Detail,
    24	        int $activeEntryCount,
    25	        bool $hasMyActiveEntry,
    26	        \DateTimeImmutable $now,
    27	    ): EventDetailButtonState {
    28	        $canApply = !$hasMyActiveEntry
    29	            && $Detail->canBeEntried($activeEntryCount, \DateTime::createFromImmutable($now));
    30	
    31	        return new EventDetailButtonState(
    32	            canApply: $canApply,
    33	            canCancel: $hasMyActiveEntry,
    34	            canDeckRegist: $Detail->canBeRegisteredDeck(),
    35	        );
    36	    }
    37	}

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	declare(strict_types=1);
     4	
     5	/*
     6	 * This file is part of EC-CUBE
     7	 *
     8	 * Copyright(c) EC-CUBE CO.,LTD. All Rights Reserved.
     9	 *
    10	 * http://www.ec-cube.co.jp/
    11	 *
    12	 * For the full copyright and license information, please view the LICENSE
    13	 * file that was distributed with this source code.
    14	 */
    15	
    16	namespace Eccube\Controller\App;
    17	
    18	use Eccube\Controller\AbstractController;
    19	use Eccube\Repository\BaseInfoRepository;
    20	use Eccube\Service\Front\Event\EventScheduleJsonBuilder;
    21	use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\JsonResponse;
    22	use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Request;
    23	use Symfony\Component\Routing\Attribute\Route;
    24	
    25	/**
    26	 * イベント大会TOP のイベントスケジュール JSON を返すエンドポイント。
    27	 * フロント側ビルド済 JS (hareruya-event.js) がブラウザから locale prefix 無しの絶対パスで fetch するため
    28	 * App namespace 配下に配置する (App namespace は locale prefix を付与しない)。
    29	 */
    30	class EventScheduleController extends AbstractController
    31	{
    32	    private const COOKIE_SHOP_KEY = 'shop';
    33	
    34	    public function __construct(
    35	        private readonly BaseInfoRepository $baseInfoRepository,
    36	        private readonly EventScheduleJsonBuilder $scheduleJsonBuilder,
    37	    ) {
    38	    }
    39	
    40	    /**
    41	     * 月別タブ専用の JSON エンドポイント。指定 YYYY-MM の 1 日〜末日のスケジュールを返す。
    42	     * フロント側 (hareruya-event.js) が 月別カレンダーで `/assets/data/events-monthly-{YYYY}-{MM}.json` を fetch するため対応する。
    43	     */
    44	    #[Route(path: '/assets/data/events-monthly-{year}-{month}.json', name: 'app_event_schedule_monthly', requirements: ['year' => '\d{4}', 'month' => '\d{2}'], methods: ['GET'])]
    45	    public function monthly(Request $request, string $year, string $month): JsonResponse
    46	    {
    47	        $Shop = $this->resolveShop($request);
    48	
    49	        $monthStart = \DateTimeImmutable::createFromFormat('!Y-m-d', sprintf('%s-%s-01', $year, $month));
    50	        if ($monthStart === false || (int) $month < 1 || (int) $month > 12) {
    51	            throw $this->createNotFoundException();
    52	        }
    53	        $monthEnd = $monthStart->modify('first day of next month');
    54	
    55	        return new JsonResponse($this->scheduleJsonBuilder->build($Shop, $monthStart, $monthEnd, $this->resolveLocale($request)));
    56	    }
    57	
    58	    #[Route(path: '/assets/data/events.json', name: 'app_event_schedule_range', methods: ['GET'])]
    59	    public function range(Request $request): JsonResponse
    60	    {
    61	        $Shop = $this->resolveShop($request);
    62	
    63	        $rangeStart = $this->parseDate((string) $request->query->get('date_from'));
    64	        $rangeEndDate = $this->parseDate((string) $request->query->get('date_to'));
    65	        if ($rangeStart === null || $rangeEndDate === null) {
    66	            throw $this->createNotFoundException();
    67	        }
    68	        $rangeEnd = $rangeEndDate->modify('+1 day');
    69	
    70	        return new JsonResponse($this->scheduleJsonBuilder->build($Shop, $rangeStart, $rangeEnd, $this->resolveLocale($request)));
    71	    }
    72	
    73	    private function parseDate(string $value): ?\DateTimeImmutable
    74	    {
    75	        $date = \DateTimeImmutable::createFromFormat('!Y-m-d', $value);
    76	        if ($date === false || $date->format('Y-m-d') !== $value) {
    77	            return null;
    78	        }
    79	
    80	        return $date;
    81	    }
    82	
    83	    private function resolveShop(Request $request): \Eccube\Entity\BaseInfo
    84	    {
    85	        $Shop = $this->baseInfoRepository->find($this->resolveShopId($request));
    86	        if ($Shop === null) {
    87	            throw $this->createNotFoundException();
    88	        }
    89	
    90	        return $Shop;
    91	    }
    92	
    93	    /**
    94	     * JSON エンドポイントは locale prefix を持たないため、fetch 元ページ (Referer) の先頭パスセグメントから locale を判定する。
    95	     * Referer 不在 / 非対応 locale の場合はリクエストの locale (既定 ja) にフォールバックする。
    96	     */
    97	    private function resolveLocale(Request $request): string
    98	    {
    99	        $path = (string) parse_url((string) $request->headers->get('referer', ''), PHP_URL_PATH);
   100	        $enabledLocales = (array) $this->getParameter('kernel.enabled_locales');
   101	        foreach (explode('/', trim($path, '/')) as $segment) {
   102	            if ($segment !== '') {
   103	                return in_array($segment, $enabledLocales, true) ? $segment : $request->getLocale();
   104	            }
   105	        }
   106	
   107	        return $request->getLocale();
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    /**
   111	     * クエリ `?shop=X` → Cookie `shop` → 既定店舗 ID の順で店舗 ID を解決する。
   112	     * イベント大会TOP の resolveShopId と同じロジック (現行踏襲)。
   113	     */
   114	    private function resolveShopId(Request $request): int
   115	    {
   116	        $queryShop = $request->query->get('shop');
   117	        if ($queryShop !== null && ctype_digit((string) $queryShop)) {
   118	            return (int) $queryShop;
   119	        }
   120	
   121	        $cookieShop = $request->cookies->get(self::COOKIE_SHOP_KEY);
   122	        if ($cookieShop !== null && ctype_digit((string) $cookieShop)) {
   123	            return (int) $cookieShop;
   124	        }
   125	
   126	        return (int) $this->eccubeConfig->get('eccube_root_base_info_id');
   127	    }

   479	    /**
   480	     * 申込可能か。 entry_flg + オンライン受付期間 + 定員 で 判定。
   481	     *
   482	     * @param int $currentEntryCount 当該詳細 への 現在 の 有効申込数 (= Repository::countActiveEntriesByDetailId 等で 取得)
   483	     * @param \DateTime|null $date 判定日時。 省略時 は 現在時刻
   484	     */
   485	    public function canBeEntried(int $currentEntryCount, ?\DateTime $date = null): bool
   486	    {
   487	        if (!$this->getEntryFlg()) {
   488	            return false;
   489	        }
   490	        $date ??= new \DateTime();
   491	        if ($this->getOnlineEntryStartDate() === null || $date < $this->getOnlineEntryStartDate()) {
   492	            return false;
   493	        }
   494	        if ($this->getOnlineEntryEndDate() === null || $date > $this->getOnlineEntryEndDate()) {
   495	            return false;
   496	        }
   497	
   498	        return $this->getCapacity() > $currentEntryCount;
   499	    }

   508	    public function canBeRegisteredDeck(): bool

   197	    {

   207	        $Detail->setOnlineEntryEndDate((clone $now)->modify('+7 days'));

   213	    }

   215	    private function attachPaymentToEvent(DtbEvent $Event): Payment
   216	    {
   217	        /** @var Payment $Payment */
   218	        $Payment = $this->getPaymentRepository()->findOneBy([], ['id' => 'ASC']);
   219	        $Event->getPayments()->add($Payment);
   220	        $this->entityManager->flush();
   221	
   222	        return $Payment;
   223	    }

   225	    private function makePaymentProcessingEntry(Customer $Customer, DtbEventDetail $Detail, Payment $Payment, string $paymentNo): DtbEventEntry
   226	    {
   227	        $EntryStatus = $this->entityManager->getReference(MtbEntryStatus::class, MtbEntryStatus::PROCESSING_PAYMENT);
   228	        $Entry = new DtbEventEntry();
   229	        $Entry->setEventDetail($Detail);
   230	        $Entry->setEntryStatus($EntryStatus);
   231	        $Entry->setPayment($Payment);
   232	        $Entry->setPrice(1000);
   233	        $Entry->setPaymentNo($paymentNo);
   234	        $Entry->setPayingCustomer($Customer);
   235	        $this->entityManager->persist($Entry);
   236	        $this->entityManager->flush();
   237	
   238	        return $Entry;
   239	    }

あなたは**指摘を反証する役**です。既定は疑う側に置いてください。

## レビュー対象（成果物）

/home/y-saito/Developments/hareruya-design-docs/design_impl_drift_report/AUDIT_0307.tsv （全件）

列: 要求ID / シート名 / 区分 / 判定 / 重要度 / 乖離種別 / 設計要件 / 実装実態 / 判定根拠

判定の詳細（設計根拠の引用・実装参照・根本原因キー）はこちら:
/home/y-saito/Developments/hareruya-design-docs/design_impl_drift_report/design_audit/0307/verdicts.tsv

## 正本（オラクル）

- 設計: /home/y-saito/Developments/hareruya-design-docs/design_impl_drift_report/design_audit/0307/sheets/<シートID>.txt
  （正本HTML設計書 0307 のシート本文を機械抽出したもの。**設計の正はここだけ**）
  レイアウト図は /home/y-saito/Developments/hareruya-design-docs/design_impl_drift_report/design_audit/0307/images/<シートID>_img*.png
- 実装: /home/y-saito/Developments/ec-cube-enterprise （ee）。**実装の正はここだけ**

## 正本でないもの

- /home/y-saito/Developments/pf-eccube3 ほかの現行ソースは移行元であって、eeの正ではない
- Excel設計書（xlsx）は上記シート本文へ変換済み。原本を読む必要はない

## 探してほしいもの（優先順）

### 1. ハルシネーション（最優先）

引用も実装参照も実在するのに、**主張だけが事実と違う**もの。機械検査では捕まりません。
すでに機械で次は確認済みなので、ここを繰り返す必要はありません。

- 設計根拠_引用が正本シート本文に逐語で実在すること（全件すべて実在）
- 実装参照が ee 内に実在するファイル:行であること（全件すべて実在）

疑ってほしいのは次です。

- 「〜していない」と書いてあるが、実は**別の場所でやっている**
  （親クラス・トレイト・イベントリスナ・サブスクライバ・ミドルウェア・
   フォームの制約・バリデータ・DBの制約・Doctrineのライフサイクル）
- 「実装が無い」と書いてあるが、**探し方が悪くて見つけられていない**だけ
- 実装の分岐の読み違い（条件の向き・early return・例外経路）
- 行番号がずれていて、引用した行が主張と関係ない

### 2. 過剰指摘

次は規約で対象外です。指摘になっていたら挙げてください。

- 実装手段の差（通信方式・認可設定・内部配線・クラス名・呼び出し順・join の種類）
- 応答にも画面にも出ない内部挙動
- 現行仕様に書かれた現行ソースの不具合・不用意な副作用（踏襲対象ではない）。
  ただし機能として定義された挙動（警告表示・並び順・据え置き時間等）は要求として扱う
- 現行仕様とリニューアル後の仕様が食い違う箇所は**後者が優先**。現行仕様側は対象外
- 廃止・フェーズ2と正本が宣言しているもの
- 見出し・表の見出し行・区分行・前置き・運用想定・背景説明
- 設計根拠_引用が**仕様を述べていない**もの（項目名・備考・表のヘッダの読み違い）

### 3. 重複

根本原因キーが違っても、実装実態が同じ欠陥を指していれば重複です。
機械で「判定＋実装実態の完全一致」は確認済みです。**文言が違う重複**を探してください。

### 4. 重要度の過大

P1/P2/P3 が業務影響と迂回可否に見合っているか。

## 指摘対象外（これを指摘しても却下します）

- 設計書の書き方・粒度・文体
- 指摘が**少なすぎる**という主張（過小報告の指摘は別途人が見ます）
- 正本シート本文に無いことを根拠にした「設計にこう書くべき」という主張
- MATCHED・OUT_OF_SCOPE・UNVERIFIABLE と判定したものの妥当性
  （成果物には載っていません。本レビューの範囲外）

## 出力形式

指摘が無ければ `NONE` の1語だけ。

指摘ごとに次を書いてください。

```
要求ID:
判定: ハルシネーション | 過剰指摘 | 重複 | 重要度過大
主張: 成果物が何と言っているか
実際: 実装の file:line を必ず示す。何行目に何と書いてあるか
修正案: 取り下げ / 判定をこう変える / 重要度をこうする / どの要求IDへ畳む
```

**重要度過大と判断したときは、修正案に「P3へ変更する」のように正しい重要度を必ず書いてください。**
書かないと修正が適用されず、重要度は過大なまま残ります。
**重複と判断したときは、修正案に畳む先の要求ID（sheet-N-RNNN）を必ず書いてください。**
```
```

---
name: design-impl-drift-audit
description: Compare HTML design specifications against an implementation to find functional drift and produce an evidence-backed HTML report. Use when Codex is asked to treat design docs as the source of truth, audit code such as controllers/services/forms/repositories/templates/batches/CSV definitions, continue a partially completed drift report, or update findings JSON/HTML for gaps between design and implementation.

# Design Impl Drift Audit

## Purpose

Use this skill to audit functional differences between HTML design specifications and an implementation repository, with the design docs treated as authoritative. Produce or continue a report that lists concrete differences with design locations, implementation locations, confidence, severity, and impact.

## Repository Discovery

1. Find the design-doc directory and implementation directory from the user request. If omitted, infer from nearby names such as `*-design-docs` and application repos.
2. Look for an existing report before starting new work. Prefer directories named `design_impl_drift_report`, existing `findings.json`, per-function `findings/*.json`, and `index.html`.
3. Count total target specs and audited specs so the report clearly states scope and remaining work.
4. Preserve existing findings. Append or refine; do not overwrite unrelated prior work.

## Audit Workflow

For each target HTML spec:

1. Identify the feature ID, route/action name, UI template, CSV/batch/API behavior, and acceptance rules from the HTML design spec.
2. Extract a requirement inventory from the HTML spec before looking at code. This inventory must include not only route/screen names, but every concrete requirement in 処理概要, 図形・テキストボックス, 項目表, 表示メッセージ, 業務ルール・計算, 入力項目, エッジケース, データ整合性, API/バッチ結果, 入出力, DB操作, バリデーション, 権限・認可, 画面遷移, エラー処理, ログ・監査, セッション, Cookie, and 排他制御. Treat bullets such as "押下時", "場合", "登録/更新/保存", "遷移", "保持", "表示/非表示", "表示切り替え", "エリアを表示", "誘導", "エラー", "判定", "連携", time limits, thresholds, and retry/idempotency rules as auditable requirements. 秒後/分後, `画面を表示してから`, 自動遷移, タイマー, and store/kiosk/account-specific forced redirects are material requirements even if the route and template already exist.
3. For every extracted requirement, create or mentally maintain a `requirementTrace` row with: `requirementId`, `designRef`, `designRequirement`, `implementationSearchTerms`, `implementationRefs`, `implementationActual`, `traceVerdict` (`MATCHED|DRIFT|NOT_FOUND|UNCERTAIN|PHASE2_EXCLUDED`), and `traceReason`. A feature cannot be marked no-drift until all material requirements have a trace verdict other than `NOT_FOUND` or unresolved `UNCERTAIN`.
4. Search the implementation with `rg` across likely files: controllers, services, repositories, form types, entities, migrations, Twig/templates, commands/batches, route definitions, JavaScript, CSV exporters/importers, and PDF/print services. Search by business terms from the requirement, route names, translation keys, DB columns, state names, constants, statuses, time values, thresholds, and side-effect verbs. For timer or auto-navigation requirements, explicitly search client and server paths for `setTimeout`, `setInterval`, millisecond/second constants such as `60000` and `60 * 1000`, `location.href`, `location.assign`, `location.replace`, redirect routes, and the condition that limits the behavior. For a negative result, also search nearby implemented flows so the report can explain why they do not satisfy the design.
5. Compare behavior and user-facing text, not just names. Check input fields, validation, defaults, search conditions, session persistence, buttons/actions, labels, button text, messages, translation keys/locales, permissions, status transitions, CSV columns, sort/order rules, scheduled timing, timeouts/expiration, thresholds, client-side timers, forced auto-navigation, side effects, idempotency, external integrations, conditional display toggles, alternate UI areas, and error handling.
6. Do not treat "route/controller/template exists" as sufficient. Existence only satisfies the route/screen requirement. It does not satisfy individual rules inside the screen, such as timeout handling, cart retention, validation branches, status transitions, DB writes, logging prohibitions, conditional hide/show behavior, alternative contact/help areas, or external API retry behavior.
7. For conditional display requirements, audit each side of the condition separately. If the HTML says "Aの場合はXを非表示、Yを表示 / A以外はXを表示、Yを非表示", record trace rows for the condition detector, X visibility, Y visibility, and the inverse condition. A nearby notice or route is not enough unless the cited template/JS actually renders the required area under the required condition.
8. For labels, button text, messages, and translation keys, do a dedicated near-match pass. Extract user-visible Japanese text from the HTML item/message tables and compare it with implementation locale values and Twig/Form usage. Treat punctuation, okurigana, missing characters, long-vowel marks, wording changes such as `商品を追加` vs `商品の追加`, and business-term changes such as `返金` vs `送金` as audit targets.
9. A text drift finding is complete only when it cites both the translation/source value and the display path that uses it, such as `messages.ja.yaml` plus the `FormType` label or Twig `|trans` call. If the implementation value exists but no display path is verified, mark it `UNCERTAIN` instead of presenting it as confirmed.
10. Do not stamp all functions as if every standard aspect was fully checked. Separate "standard audit target aspects" from actual evidence. For each finding, list the aspects actually supported by evidence; for no-drift functions, include a requirement trace summary naming the concrete HTML requirements checked and implementation refs searched.
11. Record only actionable drift. If evidence is incomplete, mark the finding `UNCERTAIN` and explain what remains ambiguous.
12. For every drift finding, split the result into implementation-ready fields: `designExpectation`, `implementationActual`, `comparisonRows`, `comparisonSummary`, `mismatchReason`, `impact`, `fixTarget`, `requiredChange`, `implementationRefs`, and, when available, short design/implementation snippets from the cited lines. Do not rely on a generic prose paragraph alone.
13. Each `comparisonRows` entry must answer all four questions explicitly: comparison item, exact design value/requirement, exact implementation value/behavior, and why those two do not match. If any of those four cells would be vague, continue investigating before recording the finding.
14. Treat phase labels such as Ph2 as auxiliary context. They can be stored, but the main report must not use phase status as a substitute for concrete behavioral differences and exact implementation locations.
15. If the HTML design spec explicitly says the whole function or scoped subfeature is `フェーズ2で実装する`, `Ph2で対応`, or `Ph1では実装しない`, do not report absence of that planned Phase 2 work as drift or as a clear implementation gap. You may keep a secondary audit note that non-implementation was confirmed, but exclude it from finding counts and implementation-gap counts. Only keep a finding when there is a concrete non-phase mismatch, such as an implemented Phase 1 path behaving differently from the design or user-facing text differing.
16. When no drift is found, add the function to the audited set with an empty findings list only after the requirement trace gate passes. If material HTML requirements are untraced, do not mark `AUDITED_NO_DRIFT_FOUND`; mark the function as requiring review or add `UNCERTAIN` findings with the unresolved requirements.
17. Do not let one confirmed finding hide other requirements in the same function. A function with `AUDITED_WITH_FINDINGS` can still have untraced material requirements; keep those requirements visible in the trace gate until each one is converted to a confirmed finding, an uncertain finding, a matched trace row, or a documented phase/design non-implementation exclusion.
18. For explicitly registered critical requirements, the harness should carry a missing-finding template or an equivalent escalation rule. If the trace is not covered by an existing finding, promote it into findings automatically or fail the quality gate loudly. Do not rely on a hand-maintained `ADDITIONAL_FINDINGS` list as the only path for user-reported or high-risk missing behavior such as timeouts, cart retention, one-minute/30-minute limits, store-account auto redirects, conditional UI areas, status transitions, external calls, or destructive side effects.
19. The HTML report must not expose broad source-signal candidates as if they were implementation gaps. A requirement-level list in the main report must be limited to confirmed or explicitly uncertain actionable drift derived from findings, such as `NOT_FOUND` or `DRIFT` requirement traces and concrete comparison rows. If a source-signal scan is used for triage, keep it out of the main HTML report unless each row has been verified against implementation source and written as a concrete finding.
20. Do not hide time-based or automatic-navigation requirements merely because they are not confirmed findings yet. Requirements involving `画面を表示してから`, seconds/minutes after display, auto redirect/navigation, or timeout-to-cart/top behavior should appear in a separate focused untraced-requirement audit queue when they remain uncovered. This queue is not a confirmed defect count; it exists so these high-risk requirements are reviewed against Controller/Twig/JavaScript/Service source instead of being buried among broad requirement-trace failures.

## Function-Level Implementation Gap Audit

For every HTML spec sheet, maintain a function-level implementation audit even when no drift finding is confirmed yet.

- Extract implementation search terms from the HTML design: route names, URL paths, translation keys, class/controller/service names, batch/API/CSV names, and high-signal business terms from the sheet title and requirement text.
- Search implementation source by function across Controller, Form, Service, Repository, Entity, Migration, Twig, Command/Batch, CSV, PDF/print, API/webhook, locale/messages, JavaScript, and configuration files.
- Store a `functionImplementationAudit` object with `verdict`, `designDerivedTerms`, `candidateRefs`, `candidateCount`, `strongSignalCount`, `searchedAreas`, and `auditNote`.
- Use separate verdicts:
  - `CONFIRMED_IMPLEMENTATION_GAP`: a concrete drift finding already proves missing implementation.
  - `NO_IMPLEMENTATION_ENTRY_FOUND`: no entry candidate was found from design-derived terms; treat as an additional-review candidate, not an automatic confirmed defect.
  - `IMPLEMENTATION_ENTRY_FOUND`: strong source evidence such as route, class, translation key, or path was found.
  - `IMPLEMENTATION_CANDIDATE_FOUND`: weak business-term candidates were found but the entry point is not proven.
  - `PHASE2_NONIMPLEMENTATION_EXPECTED`: HTML design explicitly scopes non-implementation to Phase 2/Ph1 exclusion.
  - `DESIGN_NONIMPLEMENTATION_EXPECTED`: HTML design explicitly says the function or scoped behavior is not implemented by code, such as `実装しない`, `実装不要`, or infrastructure/external-service configuration replacing application code.
- Use `DESIGN_NONIMPLEMENTATION_EXPECTED` as the function-level verdict only when the non-implementation statement applies to the whole function or the implementation entry itself, for example an external WordPress server, Docker image cron, Aurora parameter group, or a bare whole-sheet `実装しない`. If the text only excludes a scoped subfeature, UI alert, HTML hint, logging detail, partial Phase 2 item, or internal job, keep the function's source-entry verdict and mention the scoped exclusion only in the trace/audit note.
- The report must show both confirmed implementation-gap findings and function-level no-entry candidates. Do not merge these counts: implementers need confirmed defects for fixing and no-entry candidates for review planning.
- A function-level candidate is not enough to mark the detailed requirements as implemented. Requirement trace still controls no-drift status.
- Do not set report metadata such as `auditComplete` or narrative notes to "complete" merely because every sheet has a function-level implementation audit. If any material requirement trace remains unresolved, state that all sheets have function-entry coverage but requirement-level audit is incomplete.
- For infrastructure-oriented sheets such as Step Functions, Lambda, WAF/S3 jobs, cron configured outside the application, or managed database settings, distinguish "not found in the requested implementation repository" from "not implemented anywhere". A confirmed implementation-gap finding may be scoped to the audited repository, but the report must say when external infrastructure repositories or cloud configuration were not checked.

## Requirement Trace Gate

Before marking a function no-drift, verify the following:

- Every material HTML requirement has a trace row. Material means it changes behavior, data, display, validation, permission, transition, side effect, external communication, schedule, retry/idempotency, logging/audit, CSV/API/PDF/batch I/O, or user-visible text.
- Each trace row includes exact design text or a tight paraphrase plus line/anchor reference, not only the sheet title.
- Each matched row cites the implementation path and line(s) that actually execute or render the behavior. A nearby class, route, or template is not enough unless the cited code contains the relevant condition/value/side effect.
- Each missing row is converted to a confirmed or uncertain finding. Use confirmed only after searching the direct path and nearby alternative implementations.
- Missing rows remain failures even if the function already has other findings. The gate is requirement-scoped, not function-scoped.
- If an explicit critical requirement has a configured missing-finding template, regenerate the report from the harness and verify the promoted finding appears in both JSON and HTML with design value, implementation behavior, mismatch reason, fix target, and search evidence.
- If a missing row does not yet have enough evidence for a confirmed or uncertain actionable finding, do not count it as an implementation gap and do not put it in the main HTML fix list. Keep the function in an incomplete requirement-trace state, and promote the row only after implementation source has been checked enough to state the exact design requirement, exact implementation behavior, and mismatch reason.
- A generic `auditEvidence` string is not a substitute for requirement traces.
- Negative searches must list the searched terms/areas and why nearby hits do not satisfy the design.

## Finding Format

Store findings in a machine-readable JSON structure. Use this shape for each finding:

```json
{
  "id": "stable-feature-id-001",
  "functionId": "m04-01_admin_stock_stock_search_list",
  "functionName": "在庫一覧",
  "severity": "high|med|low",
  "dimension": "①機能/ルート未実装",
  "confidence": "CONFIRMED|UNCERTAIN",
  "design": {
    "file": "path/to/spec.html",
    "lines": "215-220",
    "summary": "設計書に書かれている仕様"
  },
  "implementation": {
    "file": "path/to/source.php or 不在",
    "lines": "337-344",
    "summary": "実装側の状態"
  },
  "difference": "設計と実装の具体的な差分",
  "comparisonRows": [
    {
      "item": "比較項目名",
      "design": "設計書の値・要求。列名、HTTPメソッド、パス、保存先、表示文言、条件、状態遷移などを具体値で書く",
      "implementation": "実装の値・挙動。実際の列名、メソッド、パス、保存先、文言、条件、処理順を具体値で書く",
      "mismatch": "なぜ一致しないのか。実装者が直すべき差分を一文で書く"
    }
  ],
  "requirementTrace": [
    {
      "requirementId": "stable-requirement-id",
      "designRef": "path/to/spec.html:123",
      "designRequirement": "HTML設計書の個別要求",
      "implementationSearchTerms": ["検索語", "route_name", "methodName"],
      "implementationRefs": ["src/...php:123"],
      "implementationActual": "実装で確認できた挙動、または不在判定",
      "traceVerdict": "MATCHED|DRIFT|NOT_FOUND|UNCERTAIN|PHASE2_EXCLUDED",
      "traceReason": "なぜその判定か"
    }
  ],
  "comparisonSummary": "設計はX、実装はY、したがってZが違う",
  "mismatchReason": "乖離と判断した具体的な理由",
  "impact": "ユーザー・業務・データへの影響",
  "fixTarget": "修正対象のファイル/ルート/サービス/不在箇所",

- 実装手段の差（通信方式・認可設定・内部配線・クラス名・呼び出し順・join の種類）
- 応答にも画面にも出ない内部挙動
- 現行仕様に書かれた現行ソースの不具合・不用意な副作用（踏襲対象ではない）。
  ただし機能として定義された挙動（警告表示・並び順・据え置き時間等）は要求として扱う
- 現行仕様とリニューアル後の仕様が食い違う箇所は**後者が優先**。現行仕様側は対象外
- 廃止・フェーズ2と正本が宣言しているもの
- 見出し・表の見出し行・区分行・前置き・運用想定・背景説明
- 設計根拠_引用が**仕様を述べていない**もの（項目名・備考・表のヘッダの読み違い）

### 3. 重複

根本原因キーが違っても、実装実態が同じ欠陥を指していれば重複です。
機械で「判定＋実装実態の完全一致」は確認済みです。**文言が違う重複**を探してください。

### 4. 重要度の過大

P1/P2/P3 が業務影響と迂回可否に見合っているか。

## 指摘対象外（これを指摘しても却下します）

- 設計書の書き方・粒度・文体
- 指摘が**少なすぎる**という主張（過小報告の指摘は別途人が見ます）
- 正本シート本文に無いことを根拠にした「設計にこう書くべき」という主張
- MATCHED・OUT_OF_SCOPE・UNVERIFIABLE と判定したものの妥当性
  （成果物には載っていません。本レビューの範囲外）

## 出力形式

指摘が無ければ `NONE` の1語だけ。

指摘ごとに次を書いてください。

```
要求ID:
判定: ハルシネーション | 過剰指摘 | 重複 | 重要度過大
主張: 成果物が何と言っているか
実際: 実装の file:line を必ず示す。何行目に何と書いてあるか
修正案: 取り下げ / 判定をこう変える / 重要度をこうする / どの要求IDへ畳む
```

**重要度過大と判断したときは、修正案に「P3へ変更する」のように正しい重要度を必ず書いてください。**
書かないと修正が適用されず、重要度は過大なまま残ります。
**重複と判断したときは、修正案に畳む先の要求ID（sheet-N-RNNN）を必ず書いてください。**
```
```

**実装の file:line を伴わない指摘は書かないでください。**

---
name: design-impl-drift-audit
description: Compare HTML design specifications against an implementation to find functional drift and produce an evidence-backed HTML report. Use when Codex is asked to treat design docs as the source of truth, audit code such as controllers/services/forms/repositories/templates/batches/CSV definitions, continue a partially completed drift report, or update findings JSON/HTML for gaps between design and implementation.
---

# Design Impl Drift Audit

## Purpose

Use this skill to audit functional differences between HTML design specifications and an implementation repository, with the design docs treated as authoritative. Produce or continue a report that lists concrete differences with design locations, implementation locations, confidence, severity, and impact.

## Repository Discovery

1. Find the design-doc directory and implementation directory from the user request. If omitted, infer from nearby names such as `*-design-docs` and application repos.
2. Look for an existing report before starting new work. Prefer directories named `design_impl_drift_report`, existing `findings.json`, per-function `findings/*.json`, and `index.html`.
3. Count total target specs and audited specs so the report clearly states scope and remaining work.
4. Preserve existing findings. Append or refine; do not overwrite unrelated prior work.

## Audit Workflow

For each target HTML spec:

1. Identify the feature ID, route/action name, UI template, CSV/batch/API behavior, and acceptance rules from the HTML design spec.
2. Extract a requirement inventory from the HTML spec before looking at code. This inventory must include not only route/screen names, but every concrete requirement in 処理概要, 図形・テキストボックス, 項目表, 表示メッセージ, 業務ルール・計算, 入力項目, エッジケース, データ整合性, API/バッチ結果, 入出力, DB操作, バリデーション, 権限・認可, 画面遷移, エラー処理, ログ・監査, セッション, Cookie, and 排他制御. Treat bullets such as "押下時", "場合", "登録/更新/保存", "遷移", "保持", "表示/非表示", "表示切り替え", "エリアを表示", "誘導", "エラー", "判定", "連携", time limits, thresholds, and retry/idempotency rules as auditable requirements. 秒後/分後, `画面を表示してから`, 自動遷移, タイマー, and store/kiosk/account-specific forced redirects are material requirements even if the route and template already exist.
3. For every extracted requirement, create or mentally maintain a `requirementTrace` row with: `requirementId`, `designRef`, `designRequirement`, `implementationSearchTerms`, `implementationRefs`, `implementationActual`, `traceVerdict` (`MATCHED|DRIFT|NOT_FOUND|UNCERTAIN|PHASE2_EXCLUDED`), and `traceReason`. A feature cannot be marked no-drift until all material requirements have a trace verdict other than `NOT_FOUND` or unresolved `UNCERTAIN`.
4. Search the implementation with `rg` across likely files: controllers, services, repositories, form types, entities, migrations, Twig/templates, commands/batches, route definitions, JavaScript, CSV exporters/importers, and PDF/print services. Search by business terms from the requirement, route names, translation keys, DB columns, state names, constants, statuses, time values, thresholds, and side-effect verbs. For timer or auto-navigation requirements, explicitly search client and server paths for `setTimeout`, `setInterval`, millisecond/second constants such as `60000` and `60 * 1000`, `location.href`, `location.assign`, `location.replace`, redirect routes, and the condition that limits the behavior. For a negative result, also search nearby implemented flows so the report can explain why they do not satisfy the design.
5. Compare behavior and user-facing text, not just names. Check input fields, validation, defaults, search conditions, session persistence, buttons/actions, labels, button text, messages, translation keys/locales, permissions, status transitions, CSV columns, sort/order rules, scheduled timing, timeouts/expiration, thresholds, client-side timers, forced auto-navigation, side effects, idempotency, external integrations, conditional display toggles, alternate UI areas, and error handling.
6. Do not treat "route/controller/template exists" as sufficient. Existence only satisfies the route/screen requirement. It does not satisfy individual rules inside the screen, such as timeout handling, cart retention, validation branches, status transitions, DB writes, logging prohibitions, conditional hide/show behavior, alternative contact/help areas, or external API retry behavior.
7. For conditional display requirements, audit each side of the condition separately. If the HTML says "Aの場合はXを非表示、Yを表示 / A以外はXを表示、Yを非表示", record trace rows for the condition detector, X visibility, Y visibility, and the inverse condition. A nearby notice or route is not enough unless the cited template/JS actually renders the required area under the required condition.
8. For labels, button text, messages, and translation keys, do a dedicated near-match pass. Extract user-visible Japanese text from the HTML item/message tables and compare it with implementation locale values and Twig/Form usage. Treat punctuation, okurigana, missing characters, long-vowel marks, wording changes such as `商品を追加` vs `商品の追加`, and business-term changes such as `返金` vs `送金` as audit targets.
9. A text drift finding is complete only when it cites both the translation/source value and the display path that uses it, such as `messages.ja.yaml` plus the `FormType` label or Twig `|trans` call. If the implementation value exists but no display path is verified, mark it `UNCERTAIN` instead of presenting it as confirmed.
10. Do not stamp all functions as if every standard aspect was fully checked. Separate "standard audit target aspects" from actual evidence. For each finding, list the aspects actually supported by evidence; for no-drift functions, include a requirement trace summary naming the concrete HTML requirements checked and implementation refs searched.
11. Record only actionable drift. If evidence is incomplete, mark the finding `UNCERTAIN` and explain what remains ambiguous.
12. For every drift finding, split the result into implementation-ready fields: `designExpectation`, `implementationActual`, `comparisonRows`, `comparisonSummary`, `mismatchReason`, `impact`, `fixTarget`, `requiredChange`, `implementationRefs`, and, when available, short design/implementation snippets from the cited lines. Do not rely on a generic prose paragraph alone.
13. Each `comparisonRows` entry must answer all four questions explicitly: comparison item, exact design value/requirement, exact implementation value/behavior, and why those two do not match. If any of those four cells would be vague, continue investigating before recording the finding.
14. Treat phase labels such as Ph2 as auxiliary context. They can be stored, but the main report must not use phase status as a substitute for concrete behavioral differences and exact implementation locations.
15. If the HTML design spec explicitly says the whole function or scoped subfeature is `フェーズ2で実装する`, `Ph2で対応`, or `Ph1では実装しない`, do not report absence of that planned Phase 2 work as drift or as a clear implementation gap. You may keep a secondary audit note that non-implementation was confirmed, but exclude it from finding counts and implementation-gap counts. Only keep a finding when there is a concrete non-phase mismatch, such as an implemented Phase 1 path behaving differently from the design or user-facing text differing.
16. When no drift is found, add the function to the audited set with an empty findings list only after the requirement trace gate passes. If material HTML requirements are untraced, do not mark `AUDITED_NO_DRIFT_FOUND`; mark the function as requiring review or add `UNCERTAIN` findings with the unresolved requirements.
17. Do not let one confirmed finding hide other requirements in the same function. A function with `AUDITED_WITH_FINDINGS` can still have untraced material requirements; keep those requirements visible in the trace gate until each one is converted to a confirmed finding, an uncertain finding, a matched trace row, or a documented phase/design non-implementation exclusion.
18. For explicitly registered critical requirements, the harness should carry a missing-finding template or an equivalent escalation rule. If the trace is not covered by an existing finding, promote it into findings automatically or fail the quality gate loudly. Do not rely on a hand-maintained `ADDITIONAL_FINDINGS` list as the only path for user-reported or high-risk missing behavior such as timeouts, cart retention, one-minute/30-minute limits, store-account auto redirects, conditional UI areas, status transitions, external calls, or destructive side effects.
19. The HTML report must not expose broad source-signal candidates as if they were implementation gaps. A requirement-level list in the main report must be limited to confirmed or explicitly uncertain actionable drift derived from findings, such as `NOT_FOUND` or `DRIFT` requirement traces and concrete comparison rows. If a source-signal scan is used for triage, keep it out of the main HTML report unless each row has been verified against implementation source and written as a concrete finding.
20. Do not hide time-based or automatic-navigation requirements merely because they are not confirmed findings yet. Requirements involving `画面を表示してから`, seconds/minutes after display, auto redirect/navigation, or timeout-to-cart/top behavior should appear in a separate focused untraced-requirement audit queue when they remain uncovered. This queue is not a confirmed defect count; it exists so these high-risk requirements are reviewed against Controller/Twig/JavaScript/Service source instead of being buried among broad requirement-trace failures.

## Function-Level Implementation Gap Audit

For every HTML spec sheet, maintain a function-level implementation audit even when no drift finding is confirmed yet.

- Extract implementation search terms from the HTML design: route names, URL paths, translation keys, class/controller/service names, batch/API/CSV names, and high-signal business terms from the sheet title and requirement text.
- Search implementation source by function across Controller, Form, Service, Repository, Entity, Migration, Twig, Command/Batch, CSV, PDF/print, API/webhook, locale/messages, JavaScript, and configuration files.
- Store a `functionImplementationAudit` object with `verdict`, `designDerivedTerms`, `candidateRefs`, `candidateCount`, `strongSignalCount`, `searchedAreas`, and `auditNote`.
- Use separate verdicts:
  - `CONFIRMED_IMPLEMENTATION_GAP`: a concrete drift finding already proves missing implementation.
  - `NO_IMPLEMENTATION_ENTRY_FOUND`: no entry candidate was found from design-derived terms; treat as an additional-review candidate, not an automatic confirmed defect.
  - `IMPLEMENTATION_ENTRY_FOUND`: strong source evidence such as route, class, translation key, or path was found.
  - `IMPLEMENTATION_CANDIDATE_FOUND`: weak business-term candidates were found but the entry point is not proven.
  - `PHASE2_NONIMPLEMENTATION_EXPECTED`: HTML design explicitly scopes non-implementation to Phase 2/Ph1 exclusion.
  - `DESIGN_NONIMPLEMENTATION_EXPECTED`: HTML design explicitly says the function or scoped behavior is not implemented by code, such as `実装しない`, `実装不要`, or infrastructure/external-service configuration replacing application code.
- Use `DESIGN_NONIMPLEMENTATION_EXPECTED` as the function-level verdict only when the non-implementation statement applies to the whole function or the implementation entry itself, for example an external WordPress server, Docker image cron, Aurora parameter group, or a bare whole-sheet `実装しない`. If the text only excludes a scoped subfeature, UI alert, HTML hint, logging detail, partial Phase 2 item, or internal job, keep the function's source-entry verdict and mention the scoped exclusion only in the trace/audit note.
- The report must show both confirmed implementation-gap findings and function-level no-entry candidates. Do not merge these counts: implementers need confirmed defects for fixing and no-entry candidates for review planning.
- A function-level candidate is not enough to mark the detailed requirements as implemented. Requirement trace still controls no-drift status.
- Do not set report metadata such as `auditComplete` or narrative notes to "complete" merely because every sheet has a function-level implementation audit. If any material requirement trace remains unresolved, state that all sheets have function-entry coverage but requirement-level audit is incomplete.
- For infrastructure-oriented sheets such as Step Functions, Lambda, WAF/S3 jobs, cron configured outside the application, or managed database settings, distinguish "not found in the requested implementation repository" from "not implemented anywhere". A confirmed implementation-gap finding may be scoped to the audited repository, but the report must say when external infrastructure repositories or cloud configuration were not checked.

## Requirement Trace Gate

Before marking a function no-drift, verify the following:

- Every material HTML requirement has a trace row. Material means it changes behavior, data, display, validation, permission, transition, side effect, external communication, schedule, retry/idempotency, logging/audit, CSV/API/PDF/batch I/O, or user-visible text.
- Each trace row includes exact design text or a tight paraphrase plus line/anchor reference, not only the sheet title.
- Each matched row cites the implementation path and line(s) that actually execute or render the behavior. A nearby class, route, or template is not enough unless the cited code contains the relevant condition/value/side effect.
- Each missing row is converted to a confirmed or uncertain finding. Use confirmed only after searching the direct path and nearby alternative implementations.
- Missing rows remain failures even if the function already has other findings. The gate is requirement-scoped, not function-scoped.
- If an explicit critical requirement has a configured missing-finding template, regenerate the report from the harness and verify the promoted finding appears in both JSON and HTML with design value, implementation behavior, mismatch reason, fix target, and search evidence.
- If a missing row does not yet have enough evidence for a confirmed or uncertain actionable finding, do not count it as an implementation gap and do not put it in the main HTML fix list. Keep the function in an incomplete requirement-trace state, and promote the row only after implementation source has been checked enough to state the exact design requirement, exact implementation behavior, and mismatch reason.
- A generic `auditEvidence` string is not a substitute for requirement traces.
- Negative searches must list the searched terms/areas and why nearby hits do not satisfy the design.

## Finding Format

Store findings in a machine-readable JSON structure. Use this shape for each finding:

```json
{
  "id": "stable-feature-id-001",
  "functionId": "m04-01_admin_stock_stock_search_list",
  "functionName": "在庫一覧",
  "severity": "high|med|low",
  "dimension": "①機能/ルート未実装",
  "confidence": "CONFIRMED|UNCERTAIN",
  "design": {
    "file": "path/to/spec.html",
    "lines": "215-220",
    "summary": "設計書に書かれている仕様"
  },
  "implementation": {
    "file": "path/to/source.php or 不在",
    "lines": "337-344",
    "summary": "実装側の状態"
  },
  "difference": "設計と実装の具体的な差分",
  "comparisonRows": [
    {
      "item": "比較項目名",
      "design": "設計書の値・要求。列名、HTTPメソッド、パス、保存先、表示文言、条件、状態遷移などを具体値で書く",
      "implementation": "実装の値・挙動。実際の列名、メソッド、パス、保存先、文言、条件、処理順を具体値で書く",
      "mismatch": "なぜ一致しないのか。実装者が直すべき差分を一文で書く"
    }
  ],
  "requirementTrace": [
    {
      "requirementId": "stable-requirement-id",
      "designRef": "path/to/spec.html:123",
      "designRequirement": "HTML設計書の個別要求",

      "traceVerdict": "MATCHED|DRIFT|NOT_FOUND|UNCERTAIN|PHASE2_EXCLUDED",
      "traceReason": "なぜその判定か"
    }
  ],
  "comparisonSummary": "設計はX、実装はY、したがってZが違う",
  "mismatchReason": "乖離と判断した具体的な理由",
  "impact": "ユーザー・業務・データへの影響",
  "fixTarget": "修正対象のファイル/ルート/サービス/不在箇所",

   220	    /**
   221	     * 適切なイベントバナーを取得
   222	     *
   223	     * @return DtbEvent[]
   224	     */
   225	    public function getEventBanners(int $baseInfoId, string $locale, int $limit): array
   226	    {
   227	        $qb = $this->createQueryBuilder('e');
   228	        $from = (new \DateTime())->setTime(0, 0, 0);
   229	        $qb->join('e.Details', 'ed')
   230	            ->where($qb->expr()->eq('IDENTITY(e.BaseInfo)', ':baseInfoId'))
   231	            ->andWhere($qb->expr()->eq('IDENTITY(e.Disp)', MtbDisp::DISPLAY_SHOW))
   232	            ->andWhere($qb->expr()->eq('IDENTITY(ed.Disp)', MtbDisp::DISPLAY_SHOW))
   233	            ->andWhere($qb->expr()->gte('ed.startDate', ':from'));
   234	        $locale === 'ja' ? $qb->andWhere("e.bannerUrl != ''") : $qb->andWhere("e.bannerEnUrl != ''");
   235	
   236	        $qb->setParameters(new ArrayCollection([
   237	            'from' => $from,
   238	            'baseInfoId' => $baseInfoId,
   239	        ]))
   240	            ->orderBy('ed.startDate', 'ASC')
   241	            ->setMaxResults($limit);
   242	
   243	        return $qb->getQuery()->execute();
   244	    }

要求ID: sheet-3-R084  
判定: ハルシネーション  
主張: 開催日・公開状態でイベントバナーを絞る仕組み自体が存在しないため「未実装」。  
実際: `src/Eccube/Repository/DtbEventRepository.php:225-243` の `getEventBanners()` は、イベントと日程を結合し、`:231-234` でイベント公開・日程公開・当日以降・バナーURLありを絞り込んでいる。問題は `src/Eccube/Controller/Front/EventTopController.php:55-60` がこのメソッドを使わず、`MtbBannerRepository::findWhereUrlIsNotEmpty()` を呼んでいること。  
修正案: 判定を「実装違い」に変える。実装実態を「必要な取得処理は存在するが、EventTopControllerが別のバナー取得処理を呼んでいる」に修正する。

要求ID: sheet-3-R119  
判定: 過剰指摘  
主張: 大型イベントの絞り込みタグが実装に無いため、確定した実装乖離である。  
実際: `src/Eccube/Resource/template/default/Event/index.twig:122-181` はフォーマットとルール適用度のタグだけを描画しており、実装実態自体は正しい。ただし正本の `sheet-3_img10.png` も完成したタグ領域に大型イベントタグを描いておらず、本文・項目表とレイアウト図が矛盾している。  
修正案: 判定を「設計裁定待ち」に変える。

要求ID: sheet-3-R219  
判定: 重要度過大  
主張: 月別タブの初期一覧が当日分ではなく当月全件になる差をP2としている。  
実際: `html/template/default/assets/hareruya/js/hareruya-event.js:1` の月別初期化は `renderAllEvents()` を呼ぶ一方、日付選択後は `renderEventsForSelectedDates()` に切り替わる。利用者は当日を一度選択すれば回避でき、データ更新・申込・決済には影響しない。  
修正案: P3へ変更する。

要求ID: sheet-3-R228  
判定: 過剰指摘  
主張: 当月表示中も前月ボタンが有効なため、確定した実装乖離である。  
実際: `src/Eccube/Resource/template/default/Event/index.twig:223-224` は前月・次月ボタンを同じ有効状態で描画し、`html/template/default/assets/hareruya/js/hareruya-event.js:1` も前月ボタンを無効化しない。ただし正本の `sheet-3_img24.png` も当月カレンダーで前月・次月ボタンを同じ白背景・黒矢印の有効表示としており、本文の「暗転させ押せない」と矛盾する。  
修正案: 判定を「設計裁定待ち」に変える。
